The gear carries the embedded `host` param so auth survives the hop, and shows only once onboarding is complete. `app/routes/app.tsx`, `app/routes/app.settings.tsx`.

- **LLM provider swap hardened — `/health` tells the truth and a bad key now falls back (two of three defects fixed; the third flagged for a founder call).** The Groq→Gemini swap (`a6cc108`) landed without its observability. Fixed here: (1) **`/health` reported the *intended* provider, not the *serving* one** — with `LLM_PROVIDER=groq` and no `GROQ_API_KEY`, the provider layer silently substitutes Gemini for 100% of traffic while `/health` still says `"groq"`. `buildDependencyHealth` now reports `groqConfigured` / `geminiConfigured` / `providerKeyPresent` (so `enabled:true` + `providerKeyPresent:false` = a silent substitution), and `/health` carries an `llmFallback` rolling-window signal fed by the fallback path — so a sustained fallback is visible, not just a log grep. (2) **A bad/expired Groq key (401/403) or a retired model (404) hard-failed instead of falling back** — `isLlmFallbackError` matched 429/5xx/network but not auth failures, the single most likely real Groq outage, so Gemini sat idle. Those statuses are now fallback-eligible. Exported `withUsageRecording` + added the missing test that exercises the fallback *through* the cost ledger (a Gemini-answered fallback bills as Gemini and registers on `/health`). `app/lib/llm/provider.server.js`, `app/services/deployment-health.server.js`, `app/lib/observability/llm-provider-health.server.js`, `app/routes/health.tsx`. **Held for a founder call (production-behaviour change):** the Gemini fallback *default* is `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`, whose free quota was exhausted (CHANGELOG 2026-08-07), so a Groq rate-limit falls back into a second 429; prod runs the code default (confirmed — no `LLM_FALLBACK_MODEL` env override), so changing it (recommended: `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`) alters live fallback behaviour. Pricing accuracy (the swap flipped the rates to `verified:true`) is a separate follow-up.

- **The AI fallback model is no longer a dead safety net (completes the provider-swap hardening above).** Following the founder call, the Gemini fallback *default* moved off `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` — whose free quota was exhausted on 2026-08-07 (the reason `LLM_MODEL` was already moved off it) — to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, the same non-exhausted tier. Before this, a Groq rate-limit fell back into a second 429 and hard-failed anyway; now the backup can actually answer. Prod runs the code default (no `LLM_FALLBACK_MODEL` env override), so it takes effect on deploy. (Voice transcription's default model borrows the same fallback constant, so it follows to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` too — dark + overridable via `VOICE_TRANSCRIBE_MODEL`.) `app/lib/llm/config.server.js`, `.env.example`, `tests/voice-feedback.test.mjs`.

- **Merchants can open Jefe in a full browser tab from inside Shopify.** `app.mynamejefe.com` is the standalone web app; the embedded Shopify shell now shows an "Open the app ↗" button (top-right) that signs the merchant into the same store on the web via `/standalone/auth` (which verifies the active install and mints a standalone session). Shown only once onboarding is complete, and never on the standalone surface itself. Host comes from config (`STANDALONE_APP_HOST`), not a hardcoded literal. `app/routes/app.tsx`.

- **The home no longer throws away its server render over a date mismatch.** `formatToday()`/`formatShortDate()` formatted dates with no pinned timezone, so the server (Railway, UTC) and the merchant's browser produced different calendar dates for the 00:00–01:00 London window every day — React saw a hydration mismatch (#418/#425), discarded the whole server-rendered home and re-rendered on the client (the wrong "Tuesday 11 August" header shown on the 12th). Both date helpers now pin one named `VIEWER_TIME_ZONE`, so SSR and client render identical text. Per-merchant timezone (the shop `ianaTimezone` the loader already fetches) is tracked as a follow-up so a non-UK store reads its own date. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **The home date is now computed server-side in the merchant's own store timezone — completing the fix above.** Two residuals remained after the `VIEWER_TIME_ZONE` pin: `formatToday()` still called `new Date()` at _render_ (a smaller SSR-vs-browser straddle in the seconds around store-midnight, not just the hour window), and the zone was hardcoded London. The current-day header label is now computed ONCE in the loader (`computeHomeDateLabel`), pinned to the **store's `ianaTimezone`** (the service zone for a per-merchant Shopify app — falls back to London when unknown), and passed as the `todayLabel` prop; the render-time `formatToday()` is gone. So a non-UK store reads its own date and there is no render-time `new Date()` left to mismatch. Pure, tz-pinned helpers (`computeHomeDateLabel`/`formatDateInZone`/`storeTimeZoneFromPayload`) with a determinism test that asserts per-zone correctness at the midnight straddle (so it can't pass-at-midday / fail-at-midnight). Fixed-instant labels (`formatShortDate`) got their merchant-tz pass in the Shape B home restructure (see the entry above). `app/lib/home/home-dates.js`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Memory correction works again after the home redesign — through the free-text composer.** The action-chat home redesign (PRs #65–79) orphaned the 13a Memory correction controls: the `memory.*` handlers + `beliefConfirmPriority`/authorship/`confirmState`/`sourceLine`/`statement` all still compute server-side, but nothing rendered them, so merchants could no longer confirm/correct/answer — only a bare free-text box. Per the founder's call — make it work _within the free-text composer_, not with per-belief buttons — the reachable Merchant Memory view (`?view=memory`) is now conversational: it renders Jefe's beliefs in **plain English with provenance** (the `statement`/`sourceLine` the loader already computed and this view ignored), **orders them by `confirmPriority`** (most worth confirming first), and **surfaces the open questions** only the merchant can answer. So a merchant confirms ("that's right"), corrects ("most of my sales are wholesale") or answers a question just by typing, and `memory.message` → `sendConversationMessage` interprets and commits it. No new buttons — the composer stays the single input. **Forget-by-typing is the one remaining gap:** it needs an `obsolete_belief` operation in the interpreter (chat 9's lane, coordinated) since the conversational path has no obsolete op today. **Also fixed the door:** the "See everything Jefe knows →" entry link lived inside the "What I'm watching" section, which returns null when there's nothing to watch — so on a quiet / all-clear home the memory surface was unreachable (found in live testing). The link now sits at the home level, always rendered; the reachability test was tightened to assert it's in the always-rendered composition, not just present somewhere in source. `app/components/merchant-memory-view.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

## 2026-08-11

### Changed

- **LLM traffic now starts on Groq GPT-OSS 120B with Gemini fallback.** Jefe now defaults structured merchant-memory, onboarding and action-chat generation to Groq `openai/gpt-oss-120b`, then falls back to Gemini `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` when the primary provider hits rate-limit, capacity or temporary network/server failures. The LLM cost ledger now records the provider/model that actually answered, carries published Groq and Gemini paid-tier rates, and keeps voice transcription pinned to Gemini instead of the shared text default. `app/lib/llm/{config,provider,pricing,usage-recorder,transcribe-voice}.server.js`, `app/lib/llm/providers/{groq,gemini}.server.js`, `apps/shopify/.env.example`.

- **Local iteration no longer treats every edit as a full-suite moment.** Conductor now exposes the Shopify dev server as the default local run script, the repo manifest separates local iteration from merge checks, and the operator docs now say to use hot reload/focused checks while coding and reserve `scripts/preflight.sh` for push/merge candidates. `.conductor/settings.toml`, `MANIFEST.json`, `AGENTS.md`, `HANDOVER.md`, `README.md`, `docs/ops/build-deploy-and-coordination.md`.

- **Home and action chat now match the intended brief scale.** The next-move card, status pill, reasoning row, chat header, messages and composer now use the tighter Home sizing values, so the page headline stays dominant, the recommendation card fits the first screen, and the "why this" reasoning wraps instead of cutting off what Jefe learned. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Action chat now keeps the move subtitle visible.** Opening a recommended move now preserves the plain-language subtitle under the move title, so context such as checking supplier lead times for low-cover products is not lost when a merchant talks the move through. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Action chat no longer crashes when a stale move id reaches the server.** Jefe now keeps UUID identifiers out of free-text redaction and ignores malformed recommendation ids before Prisma sees them, so asking a question inside a move chat returns a reply instead of a local Prisma UUID error. `app/lib/merchant-memory/context-retriever.server.js`.

- **Action chat now has a governed commerce analyst for quantified follow-ups.** When a merchant asks an open commerce question inside a move, Jefe can now plan bounded read-only analysis, fetch redacted non-PII commerce rows, run server-owned calculations and derive formulas before replying. Replenishment quantity questions now get an opinionated numeric answer using a stated 30-day cover default instead of “I do not have specific purchase quantities.” `app/lib/merchant-memory/{commerce-analyst,conversation,commerce-calculations}.server.js`.

- **Ops now shows live merchant state, not just event history.** The internal Ops merchant drill-down now surfaces each live shop's current health, attention items, lifecycle progress, Merchant Memory state, open questions, latest insights, generated goals, current plan, saved plan/action chat replies with their recommendation/action linkage, action ledger progress, action write outcomes, autonomy policies and existing cost/activity telemetry from read-only ledgers. `apps/ops/server.mjs`, `apps/ops/format.mjs`.

- **Action chat now answers from the plan's evidence and current Merchant Memory.** Jefe now saves a durable evidence snapshot with each Plan recommendation and uses a shared memory context retriever when merchants ask questions inside a move chat. If a plan says two products are at risk, the chat receives the actual supported product rows where Merchant Memory has them, plus the current system context, instead of stopping at the compressed recommendation summary. `prisma/schema.prisma`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/context-retriever.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/conversation.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-plan/{candidates,service}.server.js`.

- **Action chat can now calculate current commerce impact before replying.** When merchants ask quantified questions inside a move chat, Jefe now plans safe commerce calculations, runs them server-side against tenant-scoped Shopify data, and answers from computed results such as revenue at risk, units sold, stock value, margin coverage and product rankings instead of guessing or saying the context is missing. `app/lib/merchant-memory/{commerce-calculations,conversation,context-retriever}.server.js`.

- **Action chat now clears the composer after sending.** After a merchant sends a question inside a move chat, the typed text now leaves the composer once Jefe receives it, while the message remains visible in the conversation above. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Local preflight now runs DB-backed tests deterministically.** The Shopify app test command now runs each test file in its own Node process and keeps the background backfill loop disabled during tests, so worker/database suites do not leak queue state into each other while sharing the local preflight database. `package.json`.

## 2026-08-10

### Changed

- **Home now focuses on Jefe's next move instead of a dashboard.** Returning merchants now see one dominant recommendation, why Jefe chose it, what is already in progress, action history, current watching signals and goals in a quiet single-column brief. Approve, decline, defer and scope changes now happen inside a full-page action chat, and the first Plan recommendation is carried through into the action row so the First Move stays the same after onboarding and approval. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/actions/{action-resolution,dead-stock-clearance}.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/conversation.server.js`.

- **Home's new brief now matches the intended visual register more closely.** The next-move card, recommendation subtitle, reasoning row and action chat now use the same spacing, date treatment, arrows and prompt-chip rhythm as the Home design reference, so the first screen reads as one calm recommendation instead of a stretched dashboard shell. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/routes/app-home-13a.tsx`.

- **Action chat now thinks before replying.** Questions inside a move chat now go to Jefe's LLM with the action, typed preview, supporting recommendation evidence and recent thread as context, so answers can explain the actual move instead of returning canned replies. While Jefe is generating the answer, the chat shows the merchant's pending message and a visible "Thinking" state. `app/lib/merchant-memory/conversation.server.js`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Goals now preserve explicit merchant targets during refinement.** When a merchant tells Jefe to hit a concrete goal such as doubling revenue in a specific horizon, Goals generation now keeps that target visible in the proposed goal instead of softening it into generic growth language. The goal prompt now says to preserve the metric and target, and validation rejects regenerated goals that drop a detected merchant-supplied target before retrying. `app/lib/merchant-goals/constants.js`, `app/lib/merchant-goals/{prompt,schema,service}.server.js`, `tests/merchant-goals.test.mjs`.

- **Goals review now recognises natural confirmations.** Merchants no longer have to type the exact "looks good" phrase to move through the 3, 6 and 12 month goal review. Jefe now asks the LLM whether a chat message is a clear confirmation such as "proceed to next"; if it is not clearly accepting the displayed goal, the message still follows the normal goal-refinement path. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-goals/service.server.js`, `tests/merchant-goals.test.mjs`.

- **CI schema drift check now recognises the existing conversation-topic index.** The Prisma schema now names the Merchant Memory conversation topic index exactly as the shipped migration created it, so fresh CI databases no longer fail the migration diff gate on an index rename only. `prisma/schema.prisma`.

## 2026-08-07

### Changed

- **LLM traffic moved back to the app's lower-quota fallback model.** Local and example Shopify env now point `LLM_MODEL` at `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, matching the app default and giving onboarding generation a separate free Gemini quota after the `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` limit was exhausted. The same variable was updated on the production Railway `jefe` service and the service was redeployed. `apps/shopify/.env.example`.

- **Goals onboarding now confirms one checkpoint at a time.** The Goals step now walks merchants through 3, 6 and 12 month goals sequentially with a compact checkpoint trail, a single focused goal card, confirm-or-revise chat input, inline revision acknowledgement and a final confirmed roadmap summary before continuing to Plan. Revision messages still go through the existing Goals coaching service, while simple confirmations advance the checkpoint flow immediately. When a merchant changes a checkpoint, the chat locks, the affected goal card switches to a skeleton and Jefe refreshes that checkpoint plus any later dependent checkpoints before the merchant confirms; failed model refreshes now stay visible with a retry action instead of silently falling back to the previous goal copy. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-goals/service.server.js`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Onboarding chat spacing is tighter and consistent.** The shared onboarding chat composer now uses a shorter full-width single-line input with the Send button inset cleanly, so placeholder and typed text use the whole field instead of being clipped into a narrow area, and the guided Goals screen removes extra vertical gaps between checkpoint, goal card, revision note, composer and controls. `app/components/onboarding-chat.tsx`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Onboarding progress now sits left with skip setup on the right.** Every onboarding screen now uses a single top header row with the Connect → Insights → Goals → Plan progress aligned left and the whole-setup skip action aligned right, matching the focused first-run layout while removing the duplicate bottom skip link. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Onboarding insights now lead with tighter, evidence-backed signals.** The first Insights screen now waits with clearer copy while Jefe checks products, orders, customers and inventory, generates at most five findings, and shows compact number-led cards with a left-aligned "See more" for the supporting explanation. The early correction button/form is removed from onboarding so merchants see a confident first read instead of being asked to debug it. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-insights/{prompt,schema}.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-insights/constants.js`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Onboarding page moves now show immediate progress without changing headers.** Clicking through first-run setup now keeps each step's normal header stable and swaps only the body content to the destination step's loading skeleton while Jefe finishes loading the real page data, so moving from Connect to Insights, Goals, Plan, or Jefe no longer feels like a stalled click. The Connect button also shows its own loading state while Insights opens. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Goals coaching now feels like a chat instead of a form.** Merchants now guide Jefe from a centered chat bar, see their message appear as a right-aligned chat bubble, and see Jefe's interpreted reply kept inline for each turn so the two-way context stays visible. The composer clears immediately after sending, has a single square-edged input surface with the Send button inside it, and no duplicate outlines or inner active colouring. The stale-goals warning is no longer shown in this flow; the existing 3, 6 and 12 month goal cards stay in place with skeleton bodies during the update. Goal coaching is stored as interpreted direction so Jefe synthesises merchant input into sharper, grounded goals instead of copying the prompt text into the cards. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-goals/{service,prompt}.server.js`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Plan refinement now uses the same chat pattern without leaking Goals context.** The Plan screen now uses the shared onboarding chat composer, keeps the Plan evidence toggle left-aligned with Insights, clears submitted text immediately, shows the merchant message and Jefe's interpretation as a two-way thread, and removes the old boxed refinement prompt. Onboarding chat history is now scoped by durable topic (`memory`, `onboarding_goals`, `onboarding_plan`) so Goals and Plan conversations stay separate. `app/components/onboarding-chat.tsx`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/conversation.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-plan/service.server.js`, `prisma/schema.prisma`.

- **The Plan card now reads as one clear first move.** The onboarding Plan screen now presents Jefe's recommendation as a focused action card with a clear approval status, action count, start-today callout, numbered carry-out steps and a lighter evidence disclosure, while keeping the Plan chat underneath for guidance and changes. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Opening Jefe after setup now shows the home loading screen.** Accepting the Plan now immediately switches to the Jefe home frame with home-shaped loading skeletons instead of showing the Plan/onboarding loading state while the home data loads. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/AppHome13a.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/styles/jefe.css`.

- **Insight corrections now keep the last trusted cards visible while Jefe updates them.** When a merchant corrects an onboarding insight and Jefe queues a replacement insight run, the Insights step no longer drops back to the loading skeleton if there is already a completed insight set to review. The previous trusted cards stay visible, corrected cards keep their corrected state, and a banner explains that Jefe is preparing the replacement set from the merchant's correction. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

- **Routine Shopify OAuth no longer restarts an in-flight install backfill.** Re-authorising an already-active shop now preserves any existing install backfill job/status instead of resetting product, inventory or order imports back to queued. True reinstalls after uninstall still reset and re-run the import. Newly spawned child jobs now use the current timestamp for `runAfter` instead of `1970-01-01`, while remaining immediately eligible for the worker. `app/services/shopify-backfill-status.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`.

## 2026-08-06

### Added

- **Local demo-store seeding now has a dedicated 300-order profile.** Added the synthetic Shopify `local_300` profile for `jefe-local-store.myshopify.com`: 300 commercial orders over the last 365 days, 260 synthetic customers, five marked test orders, and refund coverage sized for a lighter local demo store. The operator docs now show the wipe -> seed -> app backfill path so the disposable store and local database can be rebuilt from scratch without reusing the oversized realistic fixture. Live seeding now also preflights the target token's required import scopes before writing, so a local token missing customer/order/inventory writes fails before creating a partial store. `tools/synthetic-shopify/`.

- **Conductor workspaces now copy local environment files into new branches.** Added a root `.worktreeinclude` so new Conductor workspaces carry the gitignored `.env` files for the root app, Shopify app, ops dashboard, growth/marketing apps and synthetic Shopify tool. This keeps local setup stable across workspace branches without committing secret values. `.worktreeinclude`.

- **Ops dashboard setup now has a committed local environment template.** Added `apps/ops/.env.example` with the same local Postgres URL as the Shopify app, fail-closed shared password and local port settings the internal panel expects, so a new workspace can run ops without copying real secret values. `apps/ops/.env.example`.

- **Ops dashboard local start now reads its `.env`.** Updated the ops app start/dev scripts to load `apps/ops/.env` when present, so local Basic Auth and database settings work from the copied template while Railway-injected variables still take precedence. `apps/ops/package.json`.

### Changed

- **First-run onboarding now uses Connect as the Shopify import progress screen.** Removed Channels from the onboarding step order so setup now runs Connect -> Insights -> Goals -> Plan, while the existing Slack/WhatsApp connector code remains available outside the first-run flow. The Connect screen now shows only imported commerce entities — SKUs, stock levels, customers, orders and refunds — with clean `X of Y` progress where Shopify exposes a reliable count and plain totals once complete; loading tiles now keep the skeleton state instead of showing a placeholder zero until there is real imported progress or a completed zero. The import checklist now tracks Shopify connection, SKUs, stock levels, customers, orders and refunds separately, shows every row at once, moves each incomplete import from estimating to importing, and only ticks a row when that import domain is complete. Inventory now runs immediately after SKU import, before the order job discovers customers and refunds. Connect now unlocks Insights as soon as the commerce import is complete, while Merchant Memory and LLM-generated insights keep building behind the Insights waiting state. Normal OAuth/session refreshes no longer reset completed commerce backfills; a fresh backfill is only started again after uninstall -> reinstall because Shopify webhooks were inactive while uninstalled. Generated onboarding now cascades in the background — Insights queue Goals, and Goals queue Plan — so merchants do not wait after moving to the next step. The Goals step now uses chat refinement only, with the planning-document upload removed from onboarding, and Insight cards only ask merchants to mark what is not right. Queued Insights work now keeps the loading skeleton visible, grounding rejections are labelled as rejected generated findings rather than thin Shopify data, and the worker drains immediately-ready backfill child jobs in one tick so delta sync/finalise do not wait for separate loop intervals. Insights snapshots now stay inside the configured LLM input budget, generated goal artifacts are excluded from upstream belief snapshots so the cascade stays one-way, and Plan generation gets stricter repair instructions plus an extra repair attempt so unsupported generic targets such as “toward 100%” are removed instead of failing onboarding. Revenue/month and the early “Noticing…” insight-generation line no longer appear on this dispatch screen. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/lib/onboarding/steps.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-status.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-insights/service.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-insights/candidates.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-goals/service.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-plan/service.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-plan/{candidates,prompt,schema}.server.js`.

- **Shopify local setup scopes now include order, customer and inventory writes.** Updated the Shopify app env template, app config and local setup docs so wipe/seed preflight checks have the write scopes they require: `write_orders`, `write_customers` and `write_inventory`. `apps/shopify/.env.example`, `shopify.app.toml`.

## 2026-07-31

### Changed

- **Morning-brief email — branded template in Jefe's voice.** Replaced the functional v1 render of `renderMorningBriefEmail` with the full welcome/winback design system (navy header band + logo, cream card, Georgia display headline, terracotta "Open Jefe →" CTA, "— Jefe" sign-off — never a human name) + a subtle "Where things stand" metric box. Honest by construction: the move block renders only when the sender passes a real suggested action; the metric box only when there's real 30-day data; an "all-clear" day says so plainly (no fabricated move). Reply-to-Jefe framing in the footer with the human-door address (`humanEmail`, hola@ today → team@ when provisioned). Pure fn, contract unchanged (`{subject,html,text,headers}`); the sender / scheduling / Reply-To / List-Unsubscribe stay untouched. Still dark behind `ENABLE_MORNING_BRIEF` — nothing sends until the flag flips. 5 render/contract tests green. `app/lib/email/morning-brief.server.js`.

- **Dropped the Polaris `<Frame>` left nav — one nav, full width (founder call).** The embedded app showed TWO navigations side by side: the Polaris Frame nav (Jefe / Changelog / Dev) _and_ the 13a home's own in-app rail (Brief/Queue/Horizon/Memory/Goals/Settings) — the "two navs, reads as unfinished" thing from the design brief. Removed the Frame `navigation` prop (+ the now-unused `navigationItems` / `useNavigate` / `useLocation` / `Navigation` import); the in-app rail is now the single navigation (it carries the queue count + autonomy state the Frame nav couldn't), and the app content uses the full width. `<Frame>` stays for Toast/Loading context; Dev tools remain reachable directly at `/app/dev`; the full Changelog page stays reachable via the rail's "New in Jefe". The grey top bar above is Shopify's own admin chrome around every embedded app — not ours to remove. `app/routes/app.tsx`.

### Added

- **"New in Jefe" now shows curated, merchant-facing product news — not the engineer changelog.** The app-home "New in Jefe" rail previously reshaped the engineer `CHANGELOG.md` (still dev-written even after stripping — file paths, "shouldRevalidate", session refs). It now reads the hand-written, in-Jefe's-voice entries that already power the public `/whats-new` page, extracted to a single shared source (`app/lib/notifications/whats-new.server.js`) imported by BOTH the rail loader and the `/whats-new` route so the two never drift; each rail item carries a headline + a plain-language line. The engineer-CHANGELOG rail mapper (`changelog-app-home.js`) is retired. Founder feedback from live testing ("the changelog in the web-app is for users — make it say useful things"); the visual "look better" polish is chat 11's rail-design lane. 3 tests. `app/lib/notifications/whats-new.server.js`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/routes/whats-new.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/{AppHome13a,sample}.tsx`.

- **Scheduled morning-brief sender — Phase 2 of the notifications backend, built DARK behind `ENABLE_MORNING_BRIEF` (+ existing `ENABLE_EMAIL`).** Completes the notifications feature: a worker step (`maybeSendMorningBriefs`, in the backfill loop) that, per merchant LOCAL send time, assembles the brief from REAL data (the day's suggested action + a real 30-day orders/revenue line, omitted honestly when there's nothing to say) and sends it via the shared `sendEmail`. Guardrails for the highest-consequence email path (scheduled bulk outbound): gated on **both** flags (so it never claims a day-key while sending is off — flipping `ENABLE_EMAIL` on later still sends today); `resolveDelivery` decides whether/where (enabled pref × a known `Shop.contactEmail` × the hard `isEmailUnsubscribed` opt-out); a **durable** per-(shop,category) day-key claim (`NotificationScheduleState`, compare-and-set) guarantees at-most-once per merchant-local day across restarts/deploys (unlike the in-memory digest guard); bounded batch per tick (blast-radius cap); self-catching (never trips the loop). Timezone-correct scheduling via pure `localClockFor`/`isBriefDue` (Intl, no dep) on the shop's `ianaTimezone`. **Reply-To** routes brief replies to a real inbox per #15's constraint — the human inbox while inbound is dark, the Door-A AI address once `ENABLE_INBOUND_EMAIL` (so a reply continues the SAME Jefe conversation), never pointed at a missing MX; footer keeps the labelled human door. Additive migration `20260731110000_notification_schedule_state` (FK-less guard table). The v1 email render is functional + honest ("— Jefe", never a person's name; "all clear" when there's no move; no fabricated content) — the branded template/voice is chat 2's comms lane. **Nothing sends until the founder flips `ENABLE_MORNING_BRIEF`.** 11 new tests (schedule across timezones · render · Reply-To · metric line). `app/lib/notifications/{schedule,morning-brief-sender}.server.js`, `app/lib/email/morning-brief.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`, `prisma/schema.prisma`.

- **`VITE_APP_VERSION` is now stamped into the client bundle at build — the enabler for the "new version — Refresh" banner (and Sentry client release tracking).** The client bundle _read_ `import.meta.env.VITE_APP_VERSION` (Sentry release; chat 5's `AppUpdateBanner`) but nothing ever _set_ it, so the baked value was always `undefined` — the banner's client-vs-server version compare could never fire. `vite.config.ts` now sets `VITE_APP_VERSION` (from `APP_VERSION`, else `RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA` — the **same source + order** the server's `/health` `version` uses, so it's an apples-to-apples compare) before `defineConfig`, so Vite's `loadEnv` bakes it into the client at build. Empty when neither is present (local dev / non-Railway build) → the banner + Sentry release stay inert, never a false "refresh". Verified the bake end-to-end (a fake SHA appears in the built `sentry.client` chunk). Half of the idle-tab-after-deploy safety net: this is the build-version stamping (arch lane); the guarded shell banner + poller is chat 5's half, whose `app.tsx` mount comes through the arch gate. `apps/shopify/vite.config.ts`.

- **`listActionTypes()` — single source of truth for which autonomy dials are live.** New engine-facts helper on the action registry (`action-intent.server.js`) returning `[{ actionType, live, requiredScopes }]`, where `live` = registered in `ACTION_REGISTRY` (the wired-contract) AND its `executeFlag` env is exactly `"true"` (its deliberate go-live switch — added `executeFlag` to the registry def). Deliberately **no** label/order/detail (that's design copy, owned by the surface). Lets the Settings autonomy roster derive live-vs-"soon" from one place, so a new action type's dial lights up on registration with **zero component edit**. 3 tests. `app/lib/actions/action-intent.server.js`.

- **"Jefe's been updated — Refresh" banner — the idle-tab version poller.** A returning merchant who leaves the app open on one screen keeps running the bundle their tab booted with (React Router already hard-reloads on a build-version mismatch _when they navigate_, so the only gap is a truly idle tab after a deploy). A shell-mounted poller (`app/components/app-update-banner.tsx`) compares the client bundle's baked `VITE_APP_VERSION` against the currently-deployed version at `/health` (`version`, same `RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA` basis) every 3 min + on tab focus/visibility; on a genuine mismatch it shows a dismissible Polaris `Banner` → `window.location.reload()` (reloads the embedded iframe → fresh bundle; App Bridge re-inits on the reload). Guardrails: a **both-defined guard** (prompts only when client AND server versions are both present and differ) keeps it fully inert until the `VITE_APP_VERSION` stamping deploys, and it no-ops on any `/health` error; non-nagging (dismiss is per-version). Fires from the first deploy after the stamping baseline. Mount reviewed by the architecture lane. `app/components/app-update-banner.tsx`, `app/routes/app.tsx`.

- **Fixed the 4.6s autonomy-dial lag on the live app home.** Toggling the per-action mode (Recommend / Approve / Auto) on the clearance card triggered a full-page data reload: `action.set_mode` returned a redirect, re-running the entire heavy `app._index` loader (~4.6s / 9.3 kB `.data`) just to persist a one-field preference. Now the dial submits via `useFetcher` (no navigation) with an optimistic local-state highlight, the `set_mode` handler returns data instead of redirecting, and a route-level `shouldRevalidate` skips the loader **for `action.set_mode` only** — Approve / Decline / Edit still fully revalidate (they change the proposed row / execute, so the card + "What Jefe did" feed must reload). 4.6s → instant. Diagnosed by chat 5; scoped + the optimistic-highlight flicker corrected on review. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/sections.tsx`.

- **Voice-note feedback — the server seam for the app-home "Record a voice note" rail (DARK behind `ENABLE_VOICE_FEEDBACK`).** `processVoiceFeedback(prisma, { file, … })` (`app/lib/feedback/voice-feedback.server.js`): audio Blob → Gemini transcript → redact → a `merchant_build_request` feedback event via `track()` (surfaced in the ops panel + Slack). Transcription is a **self-contained** module (`app/lib/llm/transcribe-voice.server.js` — Gemini multimodal, **no new STT vendor**; model via `VOICE_TRANSCRIBE_MODEL`, default the app model, audio-support to verify before go-live) that doesn't touch the shared text-only provider; metered through the cost ledger (`feature: voice_feedback`). **v1 = transcript-only** (founder call) — the raw audio is **not** persisted (the app has no blob storage; keeping audio is a deferred new-vendor/PII decision). Reuses the existing multipart transport + `track()` sink — no new infra, no migration. Flag unset ⇒ no-op; the client capture (`MediaRecorder`) + the `feedback.voice` intent are the surface lane (chat 11 wires Record → this seam). 8 tests. `app/lib/feedback/`, `app/lib/llm/transcribe-voice.server.js`.

- **Memory controls on the LIVE app home are now real — confirm / correct / forget / teach / answer.** The 13a Memory section's controls (rendered visible-but-inert until now, per the wire-or-keep rule) post real `memory.*` intents, handled in `app._index`'s action and dispatched to chat 9's merchant-memory services. "That's right" → `confirmBelief`; "Not quite"/Edit → a plain-English fix **validated against the belief's own definition** before a direct `correctBelief` — so a typed belief (currency / number / enum) can never be corrupted by free text; an edit that can't be parsed as that belief's type is handed to Jefe's conversation interpreter instead; Forget → `markBeliefObsolete`; "Teach Jefe" (header) → `sendConversationMessage` (→ `upsertMerchantSuppliedBelief`). Memory's **"Still guessing"** group is now sourced from the real questions/gap feed (`getOpenQuestions`, loaded in the daily loader) — not belief status — and each open question is answered inline via `memory.answer_question` (→ `sendConversationMessage`, which validates, records the belief and retracts the question). Belief **ids** posted by the UI are resolved to belief **keys** server-side, scoped to the merchant + active beliefs, so a merchant can only act on their own live memory. All of this is gated on a new `interactive` prop threaded `DailyHome → AppHome13a → Memory/Brief sections`: the live home passes it (controls are real react-router `<Form>` posts that revalidate in place, keeping the merchant on the Memory tab); the public `/app-home-13a` design preview omits it, so its controls stay exactly the inert spans they were (no fabricated posts — preview unchanged). Logging is identifier-only (belief / question ids + merchant id) — never the merchant's free-text statement/answer or customer PII (the value validator rejects emails). Open-question answers **exact-target the clicked question end to end**: `memory.answer_question` forwards `relatedOpenQuestionId` into chat 9's `sendConversationMessage` (landed `2bb8c31`), which sets it as the current open question before interpretation — so the answer + retraction land on that question, with a graceful fall back to top-priority for a stale/answered id. Depends on chat 9's projection fix (`09f5a6d`) so the wired surface renders real authorship/confirm-state. `app/components/app-home/{sections,AppHome13a,data}.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`. 10 source-level wiring tests (`tests/memory-surface-wiring.test.mjs`). Preflight green.

- **13a Horizon is now store-grounded — real run-out dates + a "Watching, not acting" block, never fabricated.** The live 13a Horizon previously rendered only the computed seasonal calendar (back-to-school → January returns). It now leads with near-term items derived from the merchant's own data: **stock run-out dates** ("Rosehip Serum runs out soon — about 5 left, selling ~1.2/day, likely out around 4 Aug", from the `inventory.low_cover_products.trailing_30d` belief = available ÷ recent daily sell-rate) and a **refund projection** ("6 refunds in the last 30 days — if the pattern holds, expect around 3 more by 14 Aug", from a merchant+shop-scoped trailing-30d refund count), with the seasonal timeline **kept** and merged in, all sorted by date. The new **"Watching, not acting"** block carries the things Jefe can see but won't act on yet, each with an _honest revisit date_: at-risk stock that lands just outside the two-week window (revisit = the day it enters the window) and a refund signal too thin to call a trend. Nothing is invented — a missing velocity means "unknown", not zero; malformed belief numbers are skipped; and a failed read **degrades to the seasonal timeline only** rather than guessing. New service `app/lib/merchant-memory/horizon.server.js` (pure, deterministic `computeHorizon` + read-only `getStoreGroundedHorizon` that reads persisted facts on the Daily Home's read-only path — no re-derivation on render); the seasonal calendar moved here from `daily-home.tsx` so all Horizon logic lives and is tested in one place. Wired additively through the daily loader → `DailyHome` → `AppHome13a` (`horizonNear` / `horizonWatching`). Closes the "store-grounded Horizon 'watching'" follow-up from the 13a flip; read-only consumer of chat 9's memory layer. 12 tests (`tests/horizon.test.mjs`). Preflight green. `app/lib/merchant-memory/horizon.server.js`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

- **TTFB now tracked alongside LCP/INP/CLS — the LCP breakdown, measurable.** The Web Vitals beacon records **TTFB** as a `web_vital` event too. It's not a Core Web Vital (so not BFS-graded) but it's the key LCP _diagnostic_: high TTFB = server-bound (the `app._index` loader), low = render-bound (fonts/CSS). So when the 3140ms LCP optimisation starts, we target the right layer from real data instead of guessing. `TRACKED_WEB_VITALS` (LCP/INP/CLS/TTFB) now gates event recording; `CORE_WEB_VITALS` stays the BFS-graded trio. `app/lib/observability/web-vitals.server.js`, `app/routes/api.web-vitals.tsx`.

- **Ops panel: "Web Vitals · BFS" — are we clearing the Built-for-Shopify bar?** New overview panel grading each Core Web Vital's **p75 over a 28-day trailing window** (`percentile_cont` over the `web_vital` events the embedded app beacons) against the BFS target — LCP ≤ 2.5s, INP ≤ 200ms, CLS ≤ 0.1 — with a ✅ / 🔴 per metric + sample size, and an "accumulating" state until there's enough data to grade. The targets live in one constant (`BFS_WEB_VITAL_TARGETS`) so aligning to Shopify's exact numbers/basis (being confirmed with growth) is a one-line change. Turns "eyeball the Partner Dashboard" into "is our admin perf BFS-passing, in our own panel." Pure `bfsWebVitalStatus` + 3 tests. `apps/ops/server.mjs`, `apps/ops/format.mjs`.

- **App-home redesign — the "13a" visual register, as an adoptable module + a live design preview (`/app-home-13a`).** The redesigned post-onboarding home (`design_handoff_jefe_app`, turn 13a) is built as a self-contained module `app/components/app-home/` (`register.ts` flat-hex WCAG-checked tokens · `primitives.tsx` · `data.ts` · `sections.tsx` · `AppHome13a.tsx`) rendering all six sections — Brief · Queue · Horizon · Memory · Goals · Settings — in the new register: one flat `#fdfbf7` surface, **hairlines not cards**, radius 4, figures in the Schibsted text face with `tabular-nums` (mono reserved for timestamps/hints/IDs), a single rust `#8c4030` accent for needs-you, and the **3px navy authorship rule** for merchant-authored / confirm-pending facts. It carries the **live clearance flow verbatim** (the wired `action.approve` / `reject` / `edit` / `set_mode` intents — the executing loop is unchanged) and closes the handoff's product gaps _in the rendering_: Memory now states plain-English facts with a per-row provenance line + authorship and the right action set — "That's right/Not quite" vs Edit/Forget vs "Tell me" (gaps #1/#2); the Brief carries real findings under the honest "nothing's on fire" sentence (gap #4); and the rail's feedback is a **real `mailto`**, not a dead SOON button (gap #3). **Not yet adopted into the live home** — `daily-home.tsx` is unchanged; it renders in a public, `noindex` design-preview route (the `/cinematic` · `/daily` pattern) with illustrative "Everdew" data for review while App Store review is in flight. Adopting it into `app._index` (and turning the six sections into real routes) is gated on a founder nod + coordination with chat 2 (owns `app._index`/`daily-home`), chat 9 (the memory pipeline must supply the plain-English statement + provenance + authorship + confirm-state for the _live_ Memory — today the module degrades honestly from `{title,value,status}`), and chat 10 (routes + the App-Bridge nav trim + review-timing). Preflight green; `daily-home.tsx` still builds as its own untouched chunk. See `QUESTIONS-FOR-MATT.md` + `docs/ops/design_backend_backlog.md` §"13a app-home rebuild". `app/components/app-home/*`, `app/routes/app-home-13a.tsx`.

- **Memory now speaks — plain-English `statement` per belief (Jefe's voice), increment 1.** The core Memory gap the 13a entry above flagged as chat-9's dependency: the surface rendered raw values, not the understanding. New `belief-statement.server.js#renderBeliefStatement(belief)` — a **deterministic** per-belief-key formatter registry (in code, not prompts) → a sentence in Jefe's voice ("3 products have stock but no sales in the last 90 days, about £4,200 tied up. The biggest is Winback Seasonal Bundle."), or **null** when no formatter exists so the 13a surface keeps its honest status-derived fallback (never a blank). A statement is exactly as strong as its belief — inference stays inference, never asserted beyond the belief's own provenance/confidence. Wired into `getMerchantMemoryView`'s projection (`statement: renderBeliefStatement(belief)`) so the live Memory view — which already consumes `statement?` — starts speaking. **Increment 1 = the seam + the highest-value exemplar (dead stock, the belief clearance acts on) + generic fallback**; the voice is now steerable on a real belief before rolling formatters across the remaining keys. 7 tests. `app/lib/merchant-memory/belief-statement.server.js`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

- **Confirm-queue prioritisation — surface the few unsure beliefs that matter, not the first 20 by key.** Matt's live testing surfaced a ~23-item confirm/correct queue at once. New `beliefConfirmPriority(status, confidence, key)` (`service.server.js`) scores each belief for a "confirm these first" queue: **0 for settled** (nothing to confirm), else **impact × uncertainty** — impact from a small high-steer key set (currency, optimisation priority, dead stock, revenue concentration, core policies) × how unsure Jefe is (lower confidence ⇒ higher). Deterministic; exposed on `getMerchantMemoryView`'s projection as `confirmPriority` so the surface shows the top-few by what's worth a merchant's eyes. Pairs with the surface-side pacing (top-few → "show more"). 4 tests. `app/lib/merchant-memory/service.server.js`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

- **Inbound email → Jefe — a merchant can reply to a Jefe email and reach Jefe (feature #15). Shipped DARK behind `ENABLE_INBOUND_EMAIL`.** Email becomes a first-class Jefe surface with two clearly-labelled doors and **zero ambiguity about who answers** (the founder's hard constraint). **Door A (`jefe@`, the AI) — full two-way:** a signature-verified inbound reply runs the _same_ conversation brain as the app + Slack (`sendConversationMessage`) and Jefe replies back out via the existing `ENABLE_EMAIL` adapter — every reply self-identifies (“This is Jefe, your AI eCommerce manager”), signs “— Jefe” (**never a human name** — permanent guardrail), and footers the human door. **Door B (`team@`, humans)** forwards to `RESEND_REPLY_TO` (matt@) and is always one labelled click away, including inside every AI-reply footer. **Verify-before-act (two-step):** Resend's `email.received` webhook is metadata-only, so the Svix-signature-verified webhook (401s anything unsigned — never acts on unauthenticated mail) gives from/to/subject/id; Door A then resolves the sender and **fetches the full email by id** (`resend.emails.receiving.get`) to get the body + the sender's SPF/DKIM/DMARC (read from the fetched `Authentication-Results` header) — a spoofed From is parked, and a stranger's mail is never even fetched (resolve precedes fetch). **Hash-only identity:** sender→shop is mapped by `sha256(email)` via a new `email_identities` index (same PII posture as unsubscribe — no plaintext), populated at `afterAuth` so a merchant is routable _before_ they churn (a post-uninstall win-back reply still finds their memory). **Idempotent + observable:** the `inbound_email_events` ledger (keyed by provider message id) means a Resend retry can't double-reply; `/health` exposes an `inboundEmail` window and the worker pages #jefe-slack on a sustained failure. **Dark by construction:** verified inbound is recorded + parked until a human flips the flag after a reviewed live round-trip — nothing auto-replies to a real merchant before then. Phase 2 adds the AI→human auto-escalation classifier (human-request / frustration / restricted-topic → team). New `app/lib/email/inbound/{signature,parse,fetch,identity,reply,health,service}.server.js`, route `app/routes/webhooks.email.inbound.tsx`, migration `20260731093000_inbound_email` (2 additive tables, drift-verified); wired into `app/shopify.server.ts` (afterAuth), `app/routes/health.tsx`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`, `.env.example`. 55 unit tests (signature / parse / auth / fetch / identity / reply / health / end-to-end routing). Provisioning (MX subdomain + Resend inbound route + the two addresses) and the `ENABLE_INBOUND_EMAIL` flip are founder/chat-5 owned — not self-flipped. Coordinated with chat 2 (outbound-template “who answers” copy) + chat 5 (DNS/Resend-inbound infra).

- **Webhook-delivery health monitoring — our-side, since the Partner % has no API.** Shopify delivers webhooks synchronously (our route processes, then ACKs), so a slow handler risks Shopify's ~5s delivery timeout — counted as a _failed delivery_ on the Partner Dashboard even though our handler logs a success, and invisible until now. The route now records each outcome + duration into an in-memory rolling window (`webhook-health.server.js`); `/health` exposes `webhooks` (received / ok / failed / slow / success-rate) so it's visible in our own health + ops panel rather than eyeballed on the Partner Dashboard; a one-off slow webhook WARNs; and the worker evaluates the window every 15 min and **pages #jefe-slack on a sustained regression** (success-rate drop or slow-webhook spike, guarded on a min volume so a quiet window can't page). This is the proactive "catch a webhook regression" monitor the Partner Dashboard can't give us programmatically (the recent 65.5% Partner failure was verified _historical_ — 0 webhook errors in Sentry 7d/30d). Pure `evaluateWebhookHealth` + 8 tests. `app/lib/observability/webhook-health.server.js`, `app/lib/ingestion/shopify/webhooks.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`, `app/routes/health.tsx`.

- **Real-user Web Vitals tracking → ops panel + Slack (LCP/INP/CLS).** The embedded app now reports its Core Web Vitals via App Bridge (`shopify.webVitals.onReport`) to a new `/api/web-vitals` beacon, which records LCP/INP/CLS as PII-free `activity_events` (topic `performance`) — so **LCP is trackable over time in the ops panel** instead of only eyeballed in the Partner Dashboard — and tier-alerts: WARN on "needs-improvement", pages #jefe-slack (deduped per-metric via the ops alerter) on a genuinely "poor" value. It's the same metric Shopify's dashboard shows (apples-to-apples), and closes the "do we auto-monitor CWV?" gap. (Webhook delivery % has no clean Shopify API — verified — so that stays on the existing per-error→#jefe-slack path.) The client reporter is embedded-only + fully guarded (a no-op on standalone / older App Bridge, so it can't break the app); the endpoint authenticates the beacon and caps metrics per report. Pure `classifyWebVital` (Google's good / needs-improvement / poor bands) + 6 tests. `app/routes/api.web-vitals.tsx`, `app/components/web-vitals-reporter.tsx`, `app/lib/observability/web-vitals.server.js`, wired in `app/routes/app.tsx`. First half of the LCP work (tracking, so the fix is measurable); the 3140ms optimisation is next.

- **Integrations phase-2: tool-stack detection feeder + orchestrator (built DARK behind `ENABLE_TOOL_STACK_DETECTION`).** The "detect the merchant's stack without asking" engine (`detectToolStack`, phase-1) now has a working Shopify-signal feeder: one bounded Admin GraphQL query (`tool-stack-signals.server.js` — recent orders' gateways+tags, customers' tags, metafield-definition namespaces) → a pure response→signals mapper → detection, orchestrated by `detectAndRecordToolStack` (`tool-stack-detection.server.js`) with the belief write left as an injected `recordBelief` seam. Flag unset ⇒ complete no-op (queries nothing, writes nothing). Seed registry expanded +9 distinctive tools (Skio/Appstle/Fera/Junip/LoyaltyLion/ShipHero/Deliverr/Sezzle/Affirm). Unwired to any surface. The `business.tool_stack` belief persistence is a Merchant Memory registry integration (chat 9's lane) — recipe + build status in `docs/integrations/tool-stack-phase2.md`. Storefront-fingerprint feeder + connect-offer surface + go-live (verify seed signatures vs real stores) are open decisions logged for the founder. Tests: `tool-stack-signals.test.mjs`, `tool-stack-detection.test.mjs`. `app/lib/integrations/`.

- **2nd executable action — `product_status_change` (archive/unarchive a product), built DARK behind `PRODUCT_STATUS_EXECUTE_ENABLED`.** Jefe's second typed action, a faithful parallel of the clearance primitive: `product-status-adapter.server.js` (`applyProductStatusChange` — flag-gated, blast-radius cap that **blocks not trims**, idempotent per-target ledger writes, compare-and-set against the live status, auto-revert on partial failure; `revertProductStatusChange` **un-gated** so undo is never trappable; pure `buildProductStatusPreview` / `computeProductStatusAutoEligibility`) + `product-status-shopify-client.server.js` (`getProductStatus` / `updateProductStatus` via the `productUpdate` mutation, API 2026-07). Reuses the generic `action_executions` / `action_execution_writes` ledger with **no migration** and the existing `write_products` scope (**no new consent**). Flag unset ⇒ the adapter hard-throws (a mis-call can never write); **UNWIRED** to any surface (no proposal emit / approve button yet). Makes the shared adapter shape concrete — see `docs/action-layer-implementation.md`. 20 tests (adapter + client). `app/lib/actions/product-status-*.server.js`.

- **LLM cost ledger — clean multi-model pricing API + the missing margin read.** The cost ledger (`llm_usage_event`) was write-only with a single-model placeholder rate. Extended `pricing.server.js` to a per-model `LLM_MODEL_PRICING` table + pure `computeLlmCostUsd({ model, inputTokens, outputTokens })` (unknown-model + non-finite-token safe), keeping `priceUsd` / `priceFor` / `LLM_PRICING` as **back-compat aliases** so the write path (`usage-recorder`) + existing tests are untouched and the change is trivially reversible. Added the margin-visibility **read** that didn't exist: `cost-report.server.js` — `summarizeLlmCost(prisma, { since, until })` groups the ledger by model × feature (`_sum` cost/tokens, `_count`) into totals + `byModel` / `byFeature`, each model carrying `rateVerified` (+ top-level `allRatesVerified`) so a spend figure is never mistaken for confirmed. **Provider is Gemini** (`gemini-3.1-flash-lite`); every rate stays `verified:false` — real per-model rates need the founder/Lewis's Gemini billing account (deliberately NOT invented — an invented rate would corrupt margin). 8 tests. `app/lib/llm/pricing.server.js`, `app/lib/llm/cost-report.server.js`, `tests/llm-cost-report.test.mjs`.

- **Executed-action visibility — the ops read over the action ledger.** Now that the action layer executes in prod, `action-report.server.js` surfaces the proposal→execution funnel + outcome mix from `action_executions`: `summarizeExecutedActions(prisma)` groups by action type × status → proposed / approved / applied / partially-applied / reverted / failed counts, a per-type breakdown, execution success-rate, and the **`hasExecutedAny` milestone** ("has Jefe done its first real successful action for a merchant yet?"). Read-only + self-contained — it does **not** touch the live execution path; wiring the milestone / a sustained-failure signal to #jefe-slack is a small worker follow-up (chat 8's alerter lane). Pure `shapeActionSummary` + a thin groupBy. 5 tests. `app/lib/actions/action-report.server.js`.

- **Integrations: `getDetectedToolStack` — the render-ready read that serves a merchant's detected stack to onboarding + an integrations page.** Over the `business.tool_stack` belief (the memory lane's half of tool-stack detection; the belief-write seam lands in parallel), `tool-stack-read.server.js` maps the detector's output into a surface-ready view: tools grouped by category, each with a plain "how Jefe spotted it" line, a confidence band + a `surfaceable` gate (tag-only 0.6 matches soften below the 0.7 firm-threshold), a natural-language headline ("Jefe spotted Klaviyo, Recharge and 3 more in your stack"), and a per-tool `connectOffer` descriptor kept visible per design but honestly gated `coming_soon`. **Nothing is marked connected** (no fabricated connection — the connect _mechanism_ is a deferred decision) and the whole read is framed `provenance:"inference"` so the surface says "Jefe spotted", never asserts fact. **NULL-SAFE:** no belief ⇒ a friendly `none_yet` view, never an error (detection is dark until `ENABLE_TOOL_STACK_DETECTION` + a caller land). Pure `buildDetectedToolStackView` core (I/O-free, unit-tested) + a thin `getDetectedToolStack(prisma,{merchantId})` wrapper; the surface (chat 11/2) renders it, the belief-seam (in flight) feeds it. 9 tests. `app/lib/integrations/tool-stack-read.server.js`. Ships with the **connect-mechanism recommendation** — hybrid, gateway-primary (an MCP gateway for the long tail + direct-OAuth for act-heavy Klaviyo, every action through the typed reversible adapter): `docs/integrations/connect-mechanism.md`.

- **`business.tool_stack` belief + `recordBelief` seam wiring — tool-stack detection now persists as Merchant Memory.** Closes the belief-write seam the phase-2 detection engine left open. A new pure leaf `app/lib/integrations/tool-stack-belief.server.js` shapes the detected stack into one belief value (`toolStackBeliefContent` — value confidence = the **strongest single matched signal**, so a weak tag-only guess never publishes as near-certain) and carries both feeder paths: (1) a **DB-derivation** — `business.tool_stack` is now a first-class registry belief (`deterministic-belief-registry`, tranche "Tool stack v1"), derived by `shopify-derivations` from signals extracted out of already-fetched records (`toolStackSignalsFromRecords`, **no new query**) and written through the normal deriver → `upsertDerivedBelief` path; and (2) the **live-query feeder** — `makeToolStackBeliefRecorder` is the concrete implementation of the orchestrator's injected `recordBelief` seam, bound to the real write path via `service.server.js#toolStackBeliefRecorder(prisma, { shopId, logger })`. The belief is **model inference**: written at systemInference precedence, rendered as inference, and superseded by any merchant correction — never merchant-confirmed. PII-safe throughout (tool ids/categories + signal kinds only). **Inert:** the `detectAndRecordToolStack` orchestrator still has no caller (dark behind `ENABLE_TOOL_STACK_DETECTION`), so the live-query seam writes nothing until that lands; the DB-derivation is signature-safe (skips unless a seed signature matches). Recipe followed from `docs/integrations/tool-stack-phase2.md`; registry count guard 132→133. Two divergences from the recipe logged for the next agent: `Order.rawPayload` is **not** currently selected in `loadDerivationContext` (order-derived gateways/tags/fulfilment stay dormant until it is — a founder-gated perf call), and feeder reconciliation (union DB + live) remains a deferred design call. 12 tests (`tool-stack-belief.test.mjs`) + registry/derivation guards. `app/lib/integrations/tool-stack-belief.server.js`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/{deterministic-belief-registry,shopify-derivations,service}.server.js`.

- **Store-hygiene ("tidy-up") scan — the 13a Brief's first _actionable_ findings (backlog item 8).** "Your call" now leads with a real, deterministic store-hygiene scan (`app/lib/store-hygiene/store-hygiene-scan.server.js`), run read-only and **deferred off the LCP-critical loader**: **refund clusters** (one product driving a disproportionate share of refunds in 30d, attributed by parsing each order's stored `refundLineItems`), **missing cost** (sellable variants with no `unitCost` — exactly the ones Jefe must skip so a markdown never drops below what they cost), **missing product type**, **missing SKU**, and **missing description**. Each emits a `Finding` carrying a **real primary action** that deep-links to the exact fix in Shopify admin (via the new canonical `adminDeepLink(shop, path)` helper — `admin.shopify.com/store/<handle>/products/…`, opened top-level out of the embedded iframe) — the previous placeholder insight→finding mapping (actionless "noticed" notes) now _fills behind_ the scan (coexist, hygiene-first, capped at 4 total so "Your call" stays calm) instead of being all there is. **Never fabricates:** a check emits only when the data is present and actually shows the gap — a store synced before the description field existed reports its description as _unknown_, never "missing" — and it **never writes** to the store (the merchant applies the fix in Shopify). To make missing-description real going forward, `PRODUCTS_QUERY` now also fetches `descriptionHtml` + `seo` (read-only, existing `read_products` scope, no new consent); it populates on the next product sync, so already-synced stores stay an honest empty until then. **Deliberately deferred (not half-built):** generative "drafted copy" + write-back is an LLM + typed write-adapter feature (the clearance pattern, a separate action-type) — v1 finds the gap and links to the fix, with honest labels ("Add a description", not a "Read the drafts" we can't yet deliver). **Deferred off the LCP-critical loader** (chat 10's critical-vs-deferred split): `DailyHome` loads it from the **`/api/store-hygiene`** resource route via `useFetcher` after first paint, so the Brief's metrics render immediately and the tidy-ups stream in a beat later — the ~5 bounded reads never sit in the blocking `app._index` `Promise.all`. Best-effort (the route returns `[]` on any failure, incl. an auth hiccup on the background fetch), so it can never delay or blank the home; `app._index` is untouched. Pure detectors + rawPayload parser, **21 tests** (20 always-run + 1 DB). `app/lib/store-hygiene/*`, `app/lib/shopify/admin-deep-link.server.js` (canonical deep-link helper, shared for Horizon etc.), `app/routes/api.store-hygiene.tsx` (deferred resource route), `app/components/daily-home.tsx` (`useFetcher`, post-paint), `app/components/app-home/sections.tsx` (`Finding.primary.external` → top-level deep-links), `app/lib/shopify/queries.server.js`.

- **Notification & communication preferences backend — and the three 13a home build-outs it makes real (in-app chat · "New in Jefe" · email-brief settings).** Adds a unified per-merchant preference layer mirroring `ActionAutonomyPolicy`: a `NotificationPreference` model (one row per `(merchant, category)`; an absent row or `null` field = the registry default, every stored value normalized on read), a deterministic category registry (`morning_brief` [schedulable] · `tidy_up` · `action_needs_approval` · `action_done` · `product_updates`), `get`/`set`/`list`, and a `resolveDelivery` seam that **composes** a preference with connected channels (`listChannelConnections`) and the email opt-out (`isEmailUnsubscribed`) rather than duplicating them. A new nullable `Shop.contactEmail`, populated once from Admin GraphQL `shop { contactEmail email }` (`ensureShopContactEmail`), gives notifications a real "to" — the app uses **offline tokens**, so `session.email` is empty in the loader and no merchant email was otherwise stored. With that backing, the three kept-to-spec placeholders on the LIVE home become real: (1) the persistent composer is now **real in-app chat with Jefe** — a new `chat.message` intent reusing the merchant-memory conversation service (`sendConversationMessage`) over a thin, write-free daily-loader read (`getDailyChatThread`), redirecting back to the home instead of the Polaris memory view; (2) **"New in Jefe"** reads the app CHANGELOG (`loadAppHomeChangelog` → the existing `changelog` prop — markdown stripped, `Internal` dropped, newest-first); (3) Settings' **"Where Jefe reaches you"** gains a real email-brief row (address + send time + inline "Change" → `notification.set`). **No fabricated data**: the old "7:30am" was hardcoded welcome-copy + prototype sample — it is now a real stored preference; the row is hidden when no contact email is known, and while scheduled delivery stays dark it says so honestly ("Not sending yet — starts when briefs go live"). **Phase 1 — preferences + honest wiring only; zero external sends** (the timezone-correct morning-brief sender in the worker is Phase 2, to land after the inbound-email session's Resend path stabilises, with `resolveDelivery` as the single shared gate). Additive migration `20260731030000_notification_preferences`; structured logging (`component:"notifications"`), the address never logged. 28 new tests; preflight green. `app/lib/notifications/*`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/{AppHome13a,sections,sample}.tsx`, `app/lib/merchant-memory/conversation.server.js`, `prisma/schema.prisma`.

### Fixed

- **Background Shopify paths now refresh the offline token before use — the durable fix for the stale-token 403 that reverted the first live clearance run.** Shopify 403s expiring offline access tokens once stale, and `authenticate.admin` only re-exchanges one on an _embedded_ request that is already within ~5 min of expiry — so pure BACKGROUND paths (the backfill worker; the clearance / autonomous write) used whatever raw token sat in the DB and 403'd when it had expired (root cause of clearance runId `4a059156` → auto-reverted). New shared `loadFreshOfflineToken(shop)` (`app/lib/shopify/offline-token.server.js`) resolves the token through `unauthenticated.admin(shop)` → the library's `ensureValidOfflineSession`, which refreshes via the stored **refresh-token grant** (no session-token JWT needed, so it works with no request in flight) and persists the fresh token. Wired into the **backfill worker** (replacing the raw `loadAccessToken`) and, via an injected `deps.loadOfflineToken` seam, the **clearance write** (the `app._index` approve path passes the refresh-capable loader; the raw read stays the default for tests + the flag-off no-op). On an unrecoverable token (no session / invalid refresh token → the merchant must reconnect) it throws a classified, WARN-logged error and the caller degrades exactly as it does for a missing token today (clearance reverts; backfill retries then fails) — never a silent stale-token write. No schema change (the `Session.refreshToken` column already exists). 4 tests. `app/lib/shopify/offline-token.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-worker.server.js`, `app/lib/actions/wire-clearance-execution.server.js`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

- **Uninstall (and every request) no longer 5xx on a Shop with a dangling merchant.** A `Shop` whose `merchantId` pointed to a missing `Merchant` (deleted by GDPR redaction / a delete-create race, with the DB-level FK not yet enforced) made `ensureShopifyTenant`'s `include: { merchant: true }` throw Prisma's _"Field merchant is required to return data, got null"_ — 5xx-ing **every** request + webhook for that shop, including `app/uninstalled`. Hit live during App-Store review (a reviewer's uninstall webhook failed; surfaced by the webhook-health monitor). `findShopifyShop` now loads the merchant separately + null-safe, and `ensureShopifyTenant` **self-heals** a dangling shop — mints + relinks a fresh `Merchant` (WARN-logged so a recurring FK gap stays visible) — instead of throwing. Resilience half; the root-cause `merchantId → Merchant` FK constraint (`onDelete: Cascade`) that stops the orphan forming is chat 10's follow-up migration. +3 tests. `app/lib/ingestion/shopify/tenant.server.js`.

- **Reinstalling a shop now clears its stale `uninstalledAt` stamp.** When an uninstalled shop reinstalled, `ensureShopifyTenant`'s reactivation set `status:"active"` but left `Shop.uninstalledAt` at its uninstall-time value — so a live, active shop kept a churn timestamp, mislabelling it as churned in retention/churn reads (the demo store `jefe-store-6u7nfi71` carried a 10:51 stamp while `active`/`ready`). The into-active **Shop** writes now clear it — the reactivation update in `tenant.server.js` and the install-backfill queue's `shop.update` both set `uninstalledAt: null` — so a reinstalled shop's stamp stays truthful to its live status (it is only re-set on the next real uninstall). `ConnectorAccount.status` transitions are deliberately untouched (that model has no `uninstalledAt`). +1 test (a reactivated shop ends `uninstalledAt`-null); a one-off data fix clears the stamp on any already-active shop that predates this. `app/lib/ingestion/shopify/tenant.server.js`, `app/services/shopify-backfill-status.server.js`. (Found by chat 9, routed via chat 10.)
- **Preflight now runs the DB-gated tests when a database is available — closing the "green-local, red-CI" gap that reddened `main` across 6 commits.** `scripts/preflight.sh`'s `npm test` step silently **skipped** every DB-gated test (the 16 ingestion / tenant / channels suites self-skip without `DATABASE_URL`), so a broken one — `f7daa0e`'s backfill-worker regression — passed local preflight and only failed in CI, landing red on `main` across 6 commits on 2026-07-31 while sessions pushed onto red unaware. Preflight now **detects a database** (an existing `DATABASE_URL`, or the canonical local `jefe-shopify-postgres` container — `npm run db:up`) and runs the DB-gated tests against it (auto `prisma migrate deploy` first): with the container up, a full local run is now **730 tests / 0 skipped** (was ~dozens silently skipped). When no DB is found it prints a **loud** skipped-tests warning with the fix instead of a silent pass. **Non-breaking:** a machine without docker is never hard-failed here — a hard-fail-on-no-DB is a deliberately deferred, coordinated step once every session has the local DB. `scripts/preflight.sh`.

- **Preflight's DB-gate no longer flakes under fleet load.** Now that the DB-gated tests actually run (above), 8+ concurrent sessions' preflight runs exhausted the shared `jefe-shopify-postgres` container's ~100-connection ceiling — a `merchant.create()` returning "Can't reach database server" — turning the merge gate flaky, which teaches re-run-till-green (the exact habit the gate exists to kill). Each run's Prisma pool is now capped (`connection_limit=5` on the auto-detected `DATABASE_URL`; the default ~17/client × ~8 concurrent test files was enough for a *single* run to near-exhaust the ceiling), and `npm run db:up` now provisions the container with `max_connections=200` (applies on the next container recreate, so no fleet disruption). Connection exhaustion, not row contention — the ingestion tests already isolate rows by unique suffix. `scripts/preflight.sh`, `apps/shopify/package.json`. (Found by chat 10.)
- **Web Vitals no longer page #jefe-slack per-event (noise fix).** The real-user Web Vitals beacon fired a Slack alert on every *"poor"* measurement — which on 2026-07-31 spammed LCP/FCP/TTFB alerts from a single cold dev-store load. Client-side vitals vary wildly by device/network, so a per-event page is noise, not signal. Per-event vitals are now **log-only** (WARN for poor/needs-improvement, INFO for good); the BFS signal is the **p75 aggregate** over 28d / 100+ calls (the ops "Web Vitals · BFS" panel). A proactive p75-breach alert is the traffic-gated follow-up. `app/routes/api.web-vitals.tsx`.

### Performance

- **`DailyHome` renders directly again — killing the React #421 that was discarding the streamed SSR home and regressing LCP on the hot daily path.** The 2026-07-30 "move 1" split (below) made `DailyHome` `React.lazy` + `<Suspense fallback={null}>` on the belief that SSR streaming + selective hydration would keep the server markup and preserve LCP. In practice it doesn't: a lazy boundary hydrates **asynchronously** — only after its chunk downloads — and in that window a stray parent / App-Bridge state update lands before the boundary finishes hydrating, tripping React **#421**. React then bails the boundary to **client rendering and throws away the streamed SSR home**, so LCP waits on JS instead of the already-painted server HTML (invisible to Sentry; visible as a console #421 + a poor LCP). Reverting `DailyHome` to a **direct static import** (no `lazy`, no `Suspense`) folds it into the `app._index` route chunk so it hydrates _synchronously_ with the route and the #421 window closes — the streamed home is retained. Because the current 13a `DailyHome` is a thin adapter over the **shared** `app-home/` module (also imported by the `/app-home-13a` preview route, so it lives in a shared chunk), un-splitting adds only the adapter to `app._index` for onboarding/memory users — **not** the whole module. Build-verified: `app._index` 50.0 → 53.1 kB raw (13.6 → 14.9 kB gzip) = **+3.0 kB / +1.3 kB gzip**, the `daily-home` chunk is removed, and the shared `index` chunk is **byte-identical** (134.13 kB) — i.e. `AppHome13a` stayed shared, only the thin adapter moved. `MerchantMemoryView` stays lazy (secondary, non-LCP path). Supersedes the LCP-preservation claim of the "move 1" entry below. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`.

### Changed

- **Settings roster is now registry-driven — a newly-graduated action's dial lights up with zero UI edit.** Follow-up to the roster build: instead of hardcoding which action types are live vs gated "Soon", the daily loader now reads the merchant's autonomy mode for each **live** type — registered in `ACTION_REGISTRY` **and** its execute-flag on, via chat 10's `listActionTypes()` (`d9d168b`) — into an `actionModes` map (the `DailyHome` prop `clearanceMode` → `actionModes`). `daily-home.tsx` keeps `ACTION_ROSTER` (design copy only: labels / detail / order + `reordering`'s needs-you prompt) and derives each row's dial-vs-"Soon"-vs-blocked from the map. So when an action graduates (a registry entry with its `executeFlag` on, e.g. `product_status_change`), its dial appears at the merchant's mode with **no component edit** — the earlier entry's manual "add its `getActionMode` read + drop `soon`" step is now automatic. **Behavioral no-op in production** (`price_markdown` is the only live type; `CLEARANCE_EXECUTE_ENABLED=true`). Engine truth (which are live) stays chat 10's lane (`listActionTypes()`); design copy stays the surface's. Preflight green. `app/routes/app._index.tsx`, `app/components/daily-home.tsx`.

- **Settings → "How much rope Jefe gets" now renders the full action roster (Tidy-ups · Listing copy · Pricing · Reordering).** Completes the "full autonomy action-type roster" follow-up from the 13a flip. Per the design + **wire-or-keep** (never wire-or-remove), the autonomy settings show **one row per action type** in the 13a roster instead of only the single live row. The one **live** type — `price_markdown` (dead-stock clearance, surfaced as "Pricing") — keeps its **real, wired** autonomy dial (`getActionMode` / `setActionMode` via `action.set_mode`, unchanged). The not-yet-live types stay **visible but honestly gated** (never a dial that can't act): `tidy_up` / `listing_copy` show a muted "soon" chip (the register's Record-tag idiom), and `reordering` keeps its real needs-you prompt ("Tell me who supplies you") — an actionable ask, not a "soon". **No fabricated numbers, no false claims** — the Pricing detail states the _real_ guardrail, "Never below what it cost you" (the clearance adapter floors at unit cost, not a margin % — there is no margin-floor belief; the design mock's "margin floor of 30%" was Everdew sample data), copy per chat 11 (design/copy owner). "Live" is grounded in the action engine (chat 9/10: `ACTION_REGISTRY` / `RESOLVERS`) — `product_status_change` has a built-but-dark adapter but isn't registered, so it is _not_ shown as a dial; when a type graduates into the registry, add its `getActionMode` read in the daily loader and set its row's `mode` (dropping `soon`). Roster order/labels/detail match `sample.ts`. Presentational only — no schema or loader-shape change (the daily loader's existing single `getActionMode(price_markdown)` read already satisfies "per live type"). `ActionPolicy` gained an optional `soon` flag; `SettingsSection` renders mode → soon-chip → blocked-prompt. Preflight green. `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/sections.tsx`.

- **App home flipped to the 13a redesign — LIVE to merchants.** The returning-merchant home (`DailyHome`) now renders the redesigned six-section app home (Brief · Queue · Horizon · Memory · Goals · Settings) in the "13a" visual register (flat surface, hairlines not cards, text-face figures, one rust accent, the 3px navy authorship rule). `DailyHome` became a thin, honest adapter over `AppHome13a`: real loader data mapped with honest fallbacks and **no fabricated numbers** — insights → Brief findings; the live **clearance suggested-action card** (mode dial + Approve→execute / Decline+reason / Adjust-%) preserved **verbatim** (same `action.*` intents, same "What Jefe did" feed honesty); Memory renders chat 9's per-belief authorship / provenance / confirm-state (the plain-English `statement` falls back to the belief title until chat 9's pass lands); the computed seasonal Horizon; and — via a small additive fetch in the daily loader — the merchant's **real per-action autonomy mode** (`getActionMode`) and **real channel connections** (`listChannelConnections`) in Settings. Rail feedback is real: Write / Email (mailto) + **Book a slot** (Calendly); "Record a voice note" is kept but honestly gated **"soon"** (the upload/transcription build is routed to chat 10). New-store and populated states both verified (honest empty: "Nothing's on fire — you're all clear."). Founder decision to ship during App Store review (developing apps are expected to iterate). Supersedes the hand-rolled "5a" build (~1.2k lines replaced by the adapter). `app/components/daily-home.tsx`, `app/components/app-home/*`, `app/routes/app._index.tsx` (loader + render). Preflight green. Follow-ups (built-to-spec-then-gate, tasked): full autonomy action-type roster, store-grounded Horizon "watching", per-goal behaviour changes, tidy-up scan with drafts, in-app composer, "New in Jefe" changelog wiring.

- **Docs resynced to reflect clearance execution LIVE (advisory → live).** `CLEARANCE_EXECUTE_ENABLED=true` has been in production since 2026-07-31, but several current-state docs still framed Jefe as advisory-only / "dark behind the flag" / awaiting go-live. Corrected the canonical docs to the now-live reality — `CLAUDE.md`, `context/11_actions_and_autonomy.md`, `context/13_action_capability_registry.md`, `HANDOVER.md`, `apps/shopify/docs/merchant-memory-state.md`, and `docs/ops/clearance-go-live.md` (marked EXECUTED; procedure + rollback retained).
